    OVERVIEW                                                                 概覽

    China East Education Holdings Limited (the “Company”) and its            按截至 2019 年 12 月 31 日止年度的平均培
    subsidiaries (collectively, the “Group”) are a leading provider in the   訓人次及產生的收入計，中國東方教育控
    vocational training education in China in terms of average students      股有限公司（「本公司」）及其附屬公司（統
    enrolled and revenue generated for the year ended 31 December            稱「本集團」）為中國領先職業技能教育提
    2019. Our business focuses on providing innovative vocational            供商。我們的業務集中在為學生提供創新
    training education to students to increase their employability in the    性職業技能教育，從而提高學生在不斷變
    ever-changing employment market, as well as supporting China’s           化的就業市場中的就業能力，並支持因中
    evolving workforce requirements driven by its sustained economic         國持續的經濟增長，城鎮化及產業升級而
    growth, urbanization and industrial upgrade.                             帶來的不斷變化的勞動力需求。


    Our vocational training education mainly covers three industry           我們的職業技能教育主要涵蓋三個行業領
    sectors, namely, culinary arts, information technology and internet      域，即烹飪技術、信息技術及互聯網技術
    technology, as well as auto services. We serve these three industry      以及汽車維修。我們於六個知名學校品牌
    sectors under six renowned school brands, namely New East                （即新東方烹飪教育、歐米奇西點西餐教
    Culinary Education, Omick Education of Western Cuisine and               育、美味學院、新華電腦教育、華信智原
    Pastry, Cuisine Academy, Xinhua Internet Technology Education,           D T 人才培訓基地及萬通汽車教育）旗下服
    Wisezone Data Technology Education and Wontone Automotive                務於該三個行業領域。截至 2019 年 12 月
    Education. As of 31 December 2019, we operated 177 schools               31 日，我們運營 177 所學校及中心，截至
    and centers with 135,571 average number of students enrolled             2019 年 12 月 31 日止年度的平均培訓人次

KEY MILESTONE
關鍵里程碑

Year             Event
年份               事件
1988-2005        •   Established New East Culinary Education, Xinhua Internet Technology Education and
                     Wontone Automotive Education.
1988 年至 2005 年   •   成立新東方烹飪教育、新華電腦教育及萬通汽車教育。

2007             •   The number of our schools in operation reached 27.
2007 年           •   運營中的學校數目達到 27 所。

2012             •   The number of our schools in operation reached 40.
2012 年           •   運營中的學校數目達到 40 所。

2015             •   The number of our schools in operation reached 71, and we expanded our business to
                     Hong Kong.
2015 年           •   運營中的學校數目達到 71 所，並將業務擴展至香港。

2016             •   Established Omick Education of Western Cuisine and Pastry.
2016 年           •   成立歐米奇西點西餐教育。

2017             •   Established Cuisine Academy.
2017 年           •   成立美味學院。

2018             •   The number of our schools and centers in operation reached 163 including 50 schools
                     under New East Culinary Education, 23 schools under Omick Education of Western
                     Cuisine and Pastry, 18 centers under Cuisine Academy, 24 schools under Xinhua Internet
                     Technology Education, 22 schools under Wisezone Data Technology Education and 26
                     schools under Wontone Automotive Education.
2018 年           •   運營中的學校及中心數目達到 163 所，包括新東方烹飪教育所轄 50 所學校、歐米奇西點
                     西餐教育所轄 23 所學校、美味學院所轄 18 個中心、新華電腦教育所轄 24 所學校、華信
                     智原 DT 人才培訓基地所轄 22 所學校及萬通汽車教育所轄 26 所學校。

June 2019        •   The Company’s shares listed on the Main Board of The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong
                     Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) on 12 June 2019.
2019 年 6 月       •   本公司股份於 2019 年 6 月 12 日在香港聯合交易所有限公司（「聯交所」）主板上市。




2019             •   The number of our schools and centers in operation reached 177 including 56 schools
                     under New East Culinary Education, 25 schools under Omick Education of Western
                     Cuisine and Pastry, 19 centers under Cuisine Academy, 25 schools under Xinhua Internet
                     Technology Education, 22 schools under Wisezone Data Technology Education and 30
                     schools under Wontone Automotive Education.
2019 年           •   運營中的學校及中心數目達到 177 所，包括新東方烹飪教育所轄 56 所學校、歐米奇西點
                     西餐教育所轄 25 所學校、美味學院所轄 19 個中心、新華電腦教育所轄 25 所學校、華信
                     智原 DT 人才培訓基地所轄 22 所學校及萬通汽車教育所轄 30 所學校。

                                                           Chairman’s Statement 主席報告



Dear Shareholders,                                                        尊敬的各位股東：


On behalf of the board (the “Board”) of directors (the “Director(s)”)     本人謹代表中國東方教育控股有限公司董
of China East Education Holdings Limited, I hereby present the            事會（「董事」）會（「董事會」），提呈本集團
annual report of the Group for the year ended 31 December 2019.           截至 2019 年 12 月 31 日止年度報告。


OVERVIEW                                                                  概述

2019 is a monumental year in the development of the Group. The            2019 年是本集團發展歷程中非常重要的一
Group was successfully listed on the Stock Exchange on 12 June            年，本集團於 2019 年 6 月 12 日在香港聯合
2019 with the largest amount ever raised in the education sector          交易所主版市場成功上市，順利登陸國際
of the world by initial public offering, and marking the Group’s          資本市場，創造了全球教育版塊有史以來
entry into the international capital market. During the year, all staff   募集資金規模最大的首次公開發行股票。
united as one, strived to implement our strategic plan and achieved       年內，全體員工團結一心、銳意進取，紮
remarkable results.                                                       實推進各項戰略部署，取得了良好業績。

                                                  and demands.

the Group to foreign exchange risk. The Group did not use any              本集團並無就對沖用途使用任何金融工

31 December 2019. However, the management monitors foreign                 有需要時考慮對沖重大外幣風險。

the PRC government. During this period, the Group conducted its            本 集 團 通 過 綫 上 辦 公 形 式， 開 展 綫 上 諮
marketing business operation through our online consultation and           詢、報名等市場運營工作，並已為中國內

     goals, we plan to pursue the following business strategies:                 現 發 展 目 標， 我 們 實 施 以 下 業 務 戰 略 計
                                                                                 劃：


     Firstly, we plan to establish five self-owned regional centers in China’s   首先，我們計劃於中國五個主要地理區域
     five major geographical regions (the “Regional Centers”), namely,           （即北京、上海、廣州、成都及西安）建立
     Beijing, Shanghai, Guangzhou, Chengdu and Xi’an. The Regional               五個自有的區域中心（「區域中心」），計劃
     Centers are expected to be equipped with advanced teaching and              為我們六個學校╱中心品牌（即新東方、歐
     practical training facilities for all of our six school/center brands,      米奇、美味學院、新華電腦、華信智原和

compensation based on each employee’s performance and                  打算在擴展業務時僱用更多教師、導師和

Company to 31 December 2019, the Company has complied with all        治守則之所有守則條文。
the code provisions set out in the CG Code.


The Board believes that good corporate governance is essential        董事會相信，為發展本集團及維護本公司
to the development of the Group and to safeguard the interests of     股東（「股東」）、潛在投資者及業務夥伴的
the shareholders of the Company (the “Shareholders”), potential       利益，維持良好企業管治實為重要，並與
investors and business partners and is consistent with the Board’s    董事會冀為股東締造價值的目標一致。本
pursuit of value creation for the Shareholders. The Company           公司致力提升適合本集團運作及發展的企
is committed to enhancing its corporate governance practices          業管治常規，並不時檢討該等常規，以確
appropriate to the conduct and the development of the Group           保本公司符合法定及專業標準，並緊貼最
and will review such practices from time to time to ensure that the   新發展。

BOARD OF DIRECTORS                                                    董事會

ROLES AND RESPONSIBILITIES                                            職務及職責

The Board assumes responsibility for leadership and control of the    董事會肩負領導及監控本公司的責任，並
Company and is collectively responsible for promoting the success     共同負責指導及監督本公司的事宜，推動
of the Company by directing and supervising the Company’s             本公司的成功。董事會應於任何時候以本
affairs. It should act in the best interests of the Company and       公司及其股東的最佳利益行事。董事會制
its Shareholders at all times. The Board sets strategies for the      定本公司的策略，並監控高級管理層的表
Company and monitors the performance and activities of the senior     現及活動。
management.


The Executive Directors of the Company are responsible for the        本公司執行董事負責本公司的日常營運，
day-to-day operations of the Company whereas the Independent          而本公司獨立非執行董事負責確保向董事
Non-executive Directors of the Company are responsible for            會及股東作出高標準之財務及管理報告，
ensuring a high standard of financial and management reporting        以及平衡董事會架構，使董事會具有高度
to the Board and Shareholders as well as to provide a balanced        獨立元素。
composition in the Board so that there is a strong independent
element on the Board.

     The Board has an overall and ongoing responsibility for the Group’s        董事會對本集團的風險管理及內部監控系

     effectiveness. It is acknowledged that risk management and internal        性。茲確認風險管理及內部監控系統乃旨
     control systems are designed to manage rather than eliminate the           在管理而非消除未能達成業務目標之風

     reasonable and not absolute assurance against material adverse             損失作出合理而非絕對的保證。
     change or damage.

     risks reported then allocates sufficient resources to address these        後則分配充足資源以處理該等風險，並監

and distribute dividends to the Shareholders to allow Shareholders to       股息，使股東能分享本公司之利潤，同時
share the Company’s profits and for the Company to retain adequate          使本公司能為未來增長保留充足儲備。
reserves for future growth.

The declaration, distribution and amount of dividend is subject to          股息的宣派、派付及金額由本公司的董事

alia, the Group’s operations, business and future development               務和未來的發展計劃、流動資金狀況、未
plans, liquidity position, our future operations and earnings, capital      來之營運和盈利、資本需求和盈餘、財務
requirements and surplus, financial condition, working capital              狀況、營運資本需求及董事會認為有關的
requirements and other factors that the Board consider relevant. The        其他因素。各財政年度之末期股息的派付
distribution of final dividend of a financial year will be subject to the   均應獲得股東的批准，惟派息金額不得超
approval of the Shareholders, but the amount of dividend shall not          過董事會的建議。
exceed the recommendation of the Board.

     NON-COMPETITION UNDERTAKING OF THE                                          控股股東的不競爭承諾
     CONTROLLING SHAREHOLDERS

     Mr. Wu Junbao, Wu Junbao Education (“Controlling                            於 2019 年 5 月 21 日， 吳 俊 保 先 生， 吳 俊
     Shareholders”), Mr. Wu Wei, Wu Wei Education, Mr. Xiao Guoqing              保教育（「控股股東」）、吳偉先生、吳偉教
     and Xiao Guoqing Education (collectively as the “Undertaking                育、肖國慶先生及肖國慶教育（統稱及各
     Parties” and each as a “Undertaking Party”), have entered into              自稱為「承諾方」）以本公司為受益人訂立
     the Deed of Non-competition on 21 May 2019 in favour of our                 不競爭契據，據此，承諾方已共同及各別
     Company, pursuant to which the Undertaking Parties have jointly             且不可撤回地向本公司（為其本身及為其

Foreign Investment Law (“FIL”)                                               《外商投資法》


On 15 March 2019, the National People’s Congress approved                    於 2019 年 3 月 15 日， 全 國 人 民 代 表 大 會
the FIL which came into effect on 1 January 2020. The FIL is                 通 過 了《 外 商 投 資 法 》， 已 自 2020 年 1 月
intended to replace the current foreign investment legal foundation          1 日起施行。《外商投資法》旨在取代中國
in the PRC consisting of three laws: the Sino-Foreign Equity Joint           目前由三項法例構成的外商投資法律基
Venture Enterprise Law, the Sino-Foreign Cooperative Joint Venture           礎：《中外合資經營企業法》、《中外合作
Enterprise Law and the Wholly Foreign-Invested Enterprise Law. For           經營企業法》及《外資企業法》。有關《外商
details of the FIL, please refer to the section headed “Regulations”         投資法》的詳情，請參閱招股章程「法規」
in the Prospectus. For details about the impact and potential                一節。有關《外商投資法》的影響及潛在後
consequences of the FIL, please also refer to the section headed             果，亦請參閱招股章程「結構性合約」一
“Structured Contracts” in the Prospectus.                                    節。

      not detecting a material misstatement resulting from fraud is           可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛

     HONG KONG FINANCIAL REPORTING STANDARDS                                 準 則》（「《香 港 財 務 報 告 準 則》」）
     (“HKFRSs”) (continued)                                                  （續）

     New and Amendments to HKFRSs that are mandatorily                       本年度強制生效的新訂及經修訂《香
     effective for the current year (continued)                                     （續）
                                                                             港財務報告準則》


     3.2   HK(IFRIC)-Int 23 Uncertainty over Income Tax                      3.2   香 港（國 際 財 務 報 告 詮 釋 委 員
           Treatments                                                              會）－詮釋第23號所得稅處理的
                                                                                   不確定性


           HK(IFRIC)-Int 23 sets out how to determine the                    香港（國際財務報告詮釋委員會）－詮
           accounting tax position when there is uncertainty over            釋第23號載列在所得稅處理方面存
           income tax treatments. The interpretation requires the            在不確定性時如何釐定會計稅務狀
           Group to determine whether uncertain tax positions                況。該詮釋要求本集團釐定是否將不
           are assessed separately or as a group and assess                  確定的稅務狀況單獨或作為一個組別
           whether it is probable that a tax authority will accept an        進行評估並評估稅務機關是否有可能

4.   SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                      4.   重大會計政策

     The consolidated financial statements have been prepared                  綜合財務報表乃按歷史成本基準編
     on the historical cost basis except for certain financial                 製，惟如下列會計政策所述於各報告
     instruments that are measured at fair values at the end of                期末按公允價值計量的若干金融工具
     each reporting period, as explained in the accounting policies            除外。
     set out below.

     Historical cost is generally based on the fair value of the               歷史成本一般根據交換商品及服務所
     consideration given in exchange for goods and services.                   付對價之公允價值計算。

     Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset           公允價值為於計量日期於市場參與者之
     or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between         有序交易中因出售資產而收取或因轉讓
     market participants at the measurement date, regardless of                負債而支付之價格，而不論該價格是否
     whether that price is directly observable or estimated using              可通過直接觀察得出或採用其他估值技
     another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value                 術進行估算。估算資產或負債的公允價
     of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the              值時，本集團會考慮市場參與者於計量
     characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants          日對資產或負債定價時所考慮之資產或
     would take those characteristics into account when pricing                負債之特點。綜合財務報表中計量及╱
     the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for            或披露的公允價值均按此基準釐定，惟
     measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated              《香港財務報告準則》第2號以股份為基
     financial statements is determined on such a basis, except for            礎的支付 規定範圍內的以股份為基礎的
     share-based payment transactions that are within the scope of             支付交易，根據《香港財務報告準則》
     HKFRS 2 Share-based Payment , leasing transactions that are               第16號（自2019年1月1日起）或《香港會
     accounted for in accordance with HKFRS 16 (since 1 January                計準則》第17號（應用《香港財務報告準
     2019) or HKAS 17 (before application of HKFRS 16), and                    則》第16號前）入賬的租賃交易，以及
     measurements that have some similarities to fair value but are            與公允價值類似但並非公允價值的計量
     not fair value, such as net realisable value in HKAS 2 Inventories        （如《香港會計準則》第2號存貨 中的可
     or value in use in HKAS 36 Impairment of Assets .                         變現淨值或《香港會計準則》第36號資
                                                                               產減值 中的使用價值）則除外。

     Basis of consolidation                                                    綜合基準


     The consolidated financial statements incorporate the                     綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本公司
     financial statements of the Company and entities (including               及其附屬公司控制的實體（包括結構
     structured entities) controlled by the Company and its                    性實體）的財務報表。本公司可通過
     subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the Company:                       以下方式實現控制：


     •     has power over the investee;                                        •      可對投資對象行使權力；


     •     is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its             •      承擔或享有參與投資對象之可
           involvement with the investee; and                                         變回報之風險或權利；及


     •     has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.             •      可對投資對象行使權力影響本
                                                                                      公司之回報。


     The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee               倘事實及情況顯示上文所述三項控制
     if facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to             因素的一項或多項出現變化，本集團
     one or more of the three elements of control listed above.                會重新評估其是否控制投資對象。


     When the Group has less than a majority of the voting rights              倘本集團於投資對象之投票權未能佔
     of an investee, it has power over the investee when the voting            大多數，但只要投票權足以賦予本集
     rights are sufficient to give it the practical ability to direct          團實際能力可單方面掌控投資對象之
     the relevant activities of the investee unilaterally. The Group           相關業務時，本集團即對投資對象擁
     considers all relevant facts and circumstances in assessing               有權力。在評估本集團於投資對象之
     whether or not the Group’s voting rights in an investee are               投票權是否足以賦予其權力時，本集
     sufficient to give it power, including:                                   團考慮所有相關事實及情況，其中包
                                                                               括：


     •     the size of the Group’s holding of voting rights relative           •      本集團持有投票權之規模相對
           to the size and dispersion of holdings of the other vote                   於其他選票持有人持有投票權
           holders;                                                                   之規模及分散性；


     •     potential voting rights held by the Group, other vote               •      本集團、其他選票持有人或其
           holders or other parties;                                                  他方持有的潛在投票權；


     •     rights arising from other contractual arrangements; and             •      其他合約安排所產生的權利；
                                                                                      及


     •     any additional facts and circumstances that indicate                •      於需要作出決定（包括先前股東
           that the Group has, or does not have, the current ability                  大會上之投票模式）時表明本集
           to direct the relevant activities at the time that decisions               團當前擁有或並無擁有指導相
           need to be made, including voting patterns at previous                     關活動之能力的任何額外事實
           shareholders’ meetings.                                                    及情況。

              impairment loss.                                                         列示。


             Revenue from contracts with customers                                     來自客戶合約的收入


              The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance                  本集團於完成履約責任時（或就此）確
              obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or             認收入，即於特定履約責任相關產
              services underlying the particular performance obligation is             品或服務的「控制權」移交客戶之時確
              transferred to the customer.                                             認。


              A performance obligation represents a good or service (or                履約責任指個別產品或服務（或一組
              a bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of           產品或服務）或一系列大致相同的個
              distinct goods or services that are substantially the same.              別產品或服務。


              Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised               控制權隨時間轉移，而倘符合以下其
              over time by reference to the progress towards complete                  中一項標準，則收入參照完全履行相
              satisfaction of the relevant performance obligation if one of            關履約責任的進度而隨時間確認：
              the following criteria is met:


              •     the customer simultaneously receives and consumes                  •    在本集團履約時客戶同時取得
                    the benefits provided by the Group’s performance as                     並耗用本集團履約所提供的利
                    the Group performs;                                                     益；


              •     the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset               •    本集團履約時，本集團的履約

              Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the             否則，收入在客戶取得個別產品或服

             Government grants                                                       政府補助


              Government grants are not recognised until there is                    在合理確保本集團將符合政府補助所
              reasonable assurance that the Group will comply with the               附條件且收到補助前，政府補助不予
              conditions attaching to them and that the grants will be               確認。
              received.


              Government grants are recognised in profit or loss on                  政府補助在本集團確認有關補助擬抵
              a systematic basis over the periods in which the Group                 銷之相關成本為支出期間按系統化基
              recognises as expenses the related costs for which the                 準於損益確認。具體而言，以要求本
              grants are intended to compensate. Specifically, government            集團購買、建造或另行收購非流動資
              grants whose primary condition is that the Group should                產為主要條件的政府補助乃於綜合財
              purchase, construct or otherwise acquire non-current assets            務狀況表確認，並於相關資產的使用
              are recognised in the consolidated statement of financial              年期內基於系統合理基準轉撥至損
              position and transferred to profit or loss on a systematic and         益。
              rational basis over the useful lives of the related assets.


              Government grants that are receivable as compensation for              政府補助是抵銷已產生的支出或虧損
              expenses or losses already incurred or for the purpose of              或旨在給予本集團實時財務支援（而
              giving immediate financial support to the Group with no future         無未來有關成本）的應收款項，於有

              Taxation                                                                   稅項


              Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently                 所得稅開支為即期應納稅款及遞延稅
              payable and deferred tax.                                                  項的總和。


              The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the               即期應納稅款按年內應課稅利潤計
              year. Taxable profit differs from profit before taxation because           算。由於收入或支出於其他年度為應
              of income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other               課稅或可扣稅，以及若干項目為不可
              years and items that are never taxable or deductible. The                  課稅或不可扣稅，故應課稅利潤與稅
              Group’s liability for current tax is calculated using tax rates            前利潤不同。本集團即期稅項負債按
              that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end                 於報告期末已頒佈或實質上已頒佈的
              of the reporting period.                                                   稅率計算。


              Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences                        遞延稅項按綜合財務報表內的資產及
              between the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the              負債賬面值與計算應課稅利潤所用相
              consolidated financial statements and the corresponding tax                應稅基之間的暫時差異確認。通常就
              bases used in the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax              所有應課稅暫時差異確認遞延稅項負
              liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary             債。倘可能有可動用可扣減暫時差異
              differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for              的應課稅利潤，則通常就所有可扣減
              all deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is              暫時差異確認遞延稅項資產。倘暫時
              probable that taxable profits will be available against which              差異是由既不影響應課稅利潤也不影
              those deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such               響會計利潤的交易中的資產及負債的
              deferred tax assets and liabilities are not recognised if the              初始確認中產生，則有關遞延稅項資
              temporary difference arises from the initial recognition of                產及負債不予確認。
              assets and liabilities in a transaction that affects neither the
              taxable profit nor the accounting profit.


              Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary              遞延稅項負債按與投資於附屬公司相
              differences associated with investments in subsidiaries                    關的應課稅暫時差異確認，惟本集團
              except where the Group is able to control the reversal of the              能夠控制暫時差異之撥回且暫時差異
              temporary difference and it is probable that the temporary                 可能於可見未來不會撥回則除外。與
              difference will not be reversed in the foreseeable future.                 該等投資相關的可扣減暫時差異所產
              Deferred tax assets arising from deductible temporary                      生的遞延稅項資產僅在可能產生充足
              differences associated with such investments are only                      應課稅利潤以利用暫時差異利益並預
              recognised to the extent that it is probable that there will be            期會在可見未來撥回的情況下確認。
              sufficient taxable profits against which to utilise the benefits of
              the temporary differences and they are expected to reverse in
              the foreseeable future.

     unit, the Group compares the carrying amount of a group                 產生單位的賬面值（包括分配至該現

     the corporate assets or portion of corporate assets allocated           企業資產的賬面值）與該組現金產生
     to that group of cash-generating units, with the recoverable            單位的可收回款項作比較。在分配減
     amount of the group of cash-generating units. In allocating             值虧損時，首先分配減值虧損以減
     the impairment loss, the impairment loss is allocated first to          少任何商譽的賬面值（如適用），然後
     reduce the carrying amount of any goodwill (if applicable)              按比例根據該單位各資產或一組現金
     and then to the other assets on a pro-rata basis based on               產生單位的賬面值分配至其他資產。
     the carrying amount of each asset in the unit or the group              資產賬面值不會減少至低於其公允價
     of cash-generating units. The carrying amount of an asset is            值減出售成本（若可計量）、使用價值
     not reduced below the highest of its fair value less costs of           （若可釐定）及零當中之最高者。本應
     disposal (if measurable), its value in use (if determinable) and        分配至該項資產的減值虧損金額，按
     zero. The amount of the impairment loss that would otherwise            比例分配至該單位其他資產或一組現
     have been allocated to the asset is allocated pro rata to the           金產生單位。減值虧損即時於損益中
     other assets of the unit or the group of cash-generating units.         確認。
     An impairment loss is recognised immediately in profit or loss.


     Where an impairment loss subsequently reverses, the                     若減值損失其後撥回，則資產（或現
     carrying amount of the asset (or a cash generating unit or the          金產生單位或一組現金產生單位）的
     group of cash-generating units) is increased to the revised             賬面值將增至其可收回金額的經修改
     estimate of its recoverable amount, but so that the increased           估計，但經增加的賬面值不得超出資
     carrying amount does not exceed the carrying amount that                產（或現金產生單位或一組現金產生
     would have been determined had no impairment loss been                  單位）於過往年度未有確認減值損失
     recognised for the asset (or a cash-generating unit or the              時原應有的賬面值。減值虧損撥回即
     group of cash-generating units) in prior years. A reversal of an        時於損益中確認。
     impairment loss is recognised immediately in profit or loss.

     The effective interest method is a method of calculating                實際利率法乃計算金融資產或金融負
     the amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability          債的攤銷成本及分配相關期間利息收
     and of allocating interest income and interest expense over             入及支出的方法。實際利率乃於初始
     the relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate            確認時按金融資產或金融負債的預計
     that exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and               年期或（如適用）較短期間，準確折現
     payments (including all fees and points paid or received                估計未來現金收款及付款（包括所有
     that form an integral part of the effective interest rate,              屬於實際利率不可或缺部分的已付或
     transaction costs and other premiums or discounts) through              已收費用及點子、交易成本及其他溢
     the expected life of the financial asset or financial liability,        價或折讓）至賬面淨值的利率。
     or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the net carrying
     amount on initial recognition.

             (ii)    Definition of default                                              (ii)    違約的定義


                     For internal credit risk management, the Group                             就內部信貸風險管理而言，本
                     considers an event of default occurs when information                      集團認為，當內部開發或從外
                     developed internally or obtained from external sources                     部來源獲得的信息表明債務人
                     indicates that the debtor is unlikely to pay its creditors,                不大可能向其債權人（包括本集
                     including the Group, in full (without taking into account                  團）作出全額支付時（不計及本
                     any collaterals held by the Group).                                        集團持有的任何抵押品），則發
                                                                                                生違約事件。


                     Irrespective of the above, the Group considers that                        儘管有上文所述，本集團認
                     default has occurred when a financial asset is more                        為，倘金融資產逾期超過90
                     than 90 days past due unless the Group has reasonable                      日，則違約已經發生，惟本集
                     and supportable information to demonstrate that a                          團有合理及可靠資料證明較寬
                     more lagging default criterion is more appropriate.                        鬆的違約標準更為適用則當別
                                                                                                論。

             (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL                                 (v)   預期信貸虧損的計量及確認


                    The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability                 預期信貸虧損的計量為違約概
                    of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the               率、違約損失率（即存在違約時
                    loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default.                的違約損失程度）及違約風險的
                    The assessment of the probability of default and loss                   函數。違約概率及違約損失率
                    given default is based on historical data adjusted by                   乃基於根據前瞻性資料調整的
                    forward-looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects                 過往數據進行評估。預期信貸
                    an unbiased and probability-weighted amount that is                     虧損的估計反映了一個無偏概
                    determined with the respective risks of default occurring               率加權金額，該金額乃以違約
                    as the weights.                                                         風險作為權重而釐定。


                    Generally, the ECL is the difference between all                        一般而言，預期信貸虧損按根
                    contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in                     據合約應付本集團的所有合約
                    accordance with the contract and the cash flows                         現金流量與本集團預計收取的
                    that the Group expects to receive, discounted at the                    現金流量（按於初始確認時釐定
                    effective interest rate determined at initial recognition.              的實際利率折現）之間的差額估
                                                                                            計。


                    Interest income is calculated based on the gross                        利息收入乃按金融資產的總賬
                    carrying amount of the financial asset unless the                       面值計算，除非金融資產發生
                    financial asset is credit impaired, in which case interest              信貸減值，於該情況下利息收
                    income is calculated based on amortised cost of the                     入則按金融資產的攤銷成本計
                    financial asset.                                                        算。


                    The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in                      本集團通過調整所有金融工具
                    profit or loss for all financial instruments by adjusting               的賬面值於損益確認該等金融
                    their carrying amount, with the exception of trade and                  工具的減值收益或虧損，惟通
                    other receivables and other financial assets measured                   過虧損撥備賬確認相應調整的
                    at amortised cost, where the corresponding adjustment                   以攤銷成本計量的貿易及其他
                    is recognised through a loss allowance account.                         應收款項以及其他金融資產例
                                                                                            外。

6.   REVENUE AND SEGMENT INFORMATION                                    6.   收入及分部資料

     The Group is mainly engaged in the provision of vocational              本集團主要在中國從事提供職業教育
     education services in the PRC.                                          服務。

     Revenue represents service income from tuition fees and                 收入指學費及服務費扣除銷售相關稅
     service fees less sales related tax, and is recognised over             項後的服務收入，其隨時間確認。
     time.

     Transaction price allocated to the remaining performance                交易價格分配至客戶合約的尚未履行
     obligation for contracts with customers                                 的履約責任

     The majority of the contracts for provision of vocational               提供職業教育服務的大多數合約期限
     education services are for periods of one year or less. As              為一年或以內。根據《香港財務報告
     permitted under HKFRS 15, the transaction price allocated to            準則》第15號的准許，分配至該等未

10. INCOME TAX EXPENSE (continued)                                           （續）
                                                                     10. 所得稅開支

    Pursuant to the Enterprise Income Tax Law and                      根據中國企業所得稅法及其實施條例
    Implementation Regulations of the Law of the PRC (the “New         （「新中國稅法」），中國附屬公司的適
    PRC Tax Law”), the applicable tax rate of PRC subsidiaries is      用稅率為25%（2018年：25%），惟若
    25% (2018: 25%), except for certain subsidiaries which are         干獲豁免納稅或享有不同優惠稅率的
    either exempted from tax or entitled to different preferential     附屬公司除外。
    tax rates.


    According to the Implementation Rules for the Law for              根據《民辦教育促進法實施條例》，
    Promoting Private Education, private schools, which are            提供學歷資格教育的民辦學校及學校
    providing academic qualification education and the school          舉辦者不要求取得合理回報的民辦學
    sponsors do not require reasonable returns, are eligible to        校有資格享受與公辦學校同等的免繳
    enjoy income tax exemption treatment as public schools,            所得稅待遇，包括石家莊新東方中等
    including Shijiazhuang New East Secondary Vocational               專業學校、江西萬通汽車技工學校、
    School (石家莊新東方中等專業學校) (“Shijiazhuang                               江西南昌新東方烹飪中專學校、江西
    Secondary”), Jiangxi Wontone Automobile Technical School           南昌新華電腦中專學校、南京萬通汽
    (江西萬通汽車技工學校), Jiangxi Nanchang New East                            車技工學校及南京烹飪（統稱為「企業
    Culinary Secondary Vocational School (江西南昌新東方烹飪                    所得稅獲豁免學校」
                                                                               ）。
    中專學校), Jiangxi Nanchang Xinhua Computer Secondary
    Vocational School (江西南昌新華電腦中專學校), Nanjing
    Wontone Automobile Vocational Technical School (南京萬通
    汽車技工學校) and Nanjing Culinary (collectively referred as
    “EIT Exempted Schools”).


    In the opinion of the directors of the Company, the Group          本公司董事認為，本集團擬將盈利業
    inclines to apply the for-profit business model to the EIT         務模式應用於企業所得稅獲豁免學
    Exempted Schools. Given that the specific taxation policies        校。鑒於根據《關於修改<中華人民共
    applicable to for-profit private schools under the Decision on     和國民辦教育促進法>的決定》，適用
    Amending the Law for Promoting Private Education of the            於營利性民辦學校的具體稅收政策尚
    PRC are yet to be introduced, the EIT Exempted Schools             未出台，所以企業所得稅獲豁免學校
    may be subject to PRC enterprise income tax going forward.         今後或需繳納中國企業所得稅。

12. DISCONTINUED OPERATION                                        12. 已終止經營業務

   In October and November 2018, the Group disposed                 於2018年10月及11月，本集團出售
   of six subsidiaries which provided training courses for          六家提供駕駛考試培訓課程的附屬公
   driving examinations. Five out of these subsidiaries were        司。其中有五家附屬公司被以每家人
   disposed to companies controlled by the Controlling              民幣10,000元的現金對價出售予控股
   Equity Holders at cash consideration of RMB10,000 each           權益持有人控制的公司，一家被以人
   and one was disposed to the independent third parties at         民幣1,000,000元的現金對價出售予
   cash consideration of RMB1,000,000. The disposals were           獨立第三方。該等出售符合本集團將
   consistent with the Group’s long-term policy to focus its        活動重點放在職業培訓教育機構運營
   activities on the operation of vocational training education     的長期戰略。
   institutions.

    For both years, the Group leases various properties, including          本集團於兩年內租賃多項物業，包括

    operations. Lease contracts are entered into for fixed term             途。租賃合約以固定期限11個月至20
    of 11 months to 20 years. Lease terms are negotiated on an              年訂立。租期視乎個案釐定，涵蓋一
    individual basis and contain a wide range of different terms            系列條款及條件。本集團於釐定租期
    and conditions. In determining the lease term and assessing             及評估據不可撤銷期時，應用合約的
    the length of the non-cancellable period, the Group applies             定義以釐定可以執行合約的期間。租
    the definition of a contract and determines the period for              賃合約概無可以強制執行的延期及終
    which the contract is enforceable. There are no enforceable             止選擇權。
    extension and termination options in lease contracts.


    The leasehold lands are amortised on a straight-line basis              租賃土地在50年期間按直線法攤銷，
    over a period of 50 years, which is based on the lease terms            該期間乃基於租期或管理層經參考中
    or estimated by the management with reference to the normal             國的常規租期估計得出。
    terms in the PRC.

                                                                       19. 遞延稅項資產

    At 31 December 2019, the Group has unrecognised                       於2019年12月31日，本集團的
    deductible temporary differences of RMB107,928,000                    未確認可扣稅暫時性差額人民幣
    (2018: RMB40,948,000). In the opinion of the directors                1 0 7 , 9 2 8 , 0 0 0 元（2 0 1 8 年 ： 人 民 幣
    of the Company, no deferred tax asset is recognised in                40,948,000元）。本公司董事認為，
    relation to such deductible temporary differences as it is not        由於不可能出現應課稅溢利可用作抵
    probable that taxable profit will be available against which the      銷可扣稅暫時性差額，故並無就該等

    available without undue cost or effort.                            可獲得的前瞻性資料作出調整。

28. SHARE OPTION SCHEMES                                              28. 購股權計劃

   The Company has adopted two share option schemes,                    本公司已採納兩項購股權計劃，即
   namely pre initial public offering share option scheme (the          首次公開發售前購股權計劃（「首次公
   “Pre-IPO Share Option Scheme”) and post initial public               開發售前購股權計劃」）及首次公 開
   offering share option scheme (the “Share Option Scheme”)             發 售 後 購 股 權 計 劃（

   (collectively referred as the “Schemes”).                            劃」
                                                                         ）（統稱為「該等計劃」
                                                                                   ）。


   The Pre-IPO Share Option Scheme                                      首次公開發售前購股權計劃


   The Pre-IPO Share Option Scheme was adopted pursuant                 根據於2018年12月7日通過的一項決
   to a resolution passed on 7 December 2018 for the primary            議案採納首次公開發售前購股權計
   purpose of providing incentives to eligible employees,               劃，該計劃主要旨在鼓勵合資格僱員
   including directors of the Company, its subsidiaries and             （包括本公司、其附屬公司及綜合聯
   consolidated affiliated entities, to subscribe for shares in the     屬實體董事）認購本公司的股份。
   Company.

   options granted must be taken up within 28 days from the             款，授出的購股權必須於授出日期起

   number of shares, which may be issued upon exercise of all           港元。根據該等計劃及本集團任何其
   options granted under the Schemes and any other schemes              他計劃授出的所有購股權獲行使時可
   of the Group, shall not in aggregate exceed 10% of the               能發行的最高股份數目合共不得超過
   shares in issue as at the date on which dealings in the shares       股份首次開始於聯交所交易當日已發
   first commence on the Stock Exchange, excluding shares               行股份的10%，不包括本公司授出的
   which may fall to be issued upon the exercise of any over-           任何超額配股權獲行使時可能發行的
   allotment option granted by the Company.                             股份。


   The options may be exercised in accordance with the terms            購股權可於董事會釐定的行使期內任
   of the Pre-IPO Share Option Scheme at any time during the            何時間根據首次公開發售前購股權計
   exercise period determined by the board of directors, which          劃條款行使，無論如何不得超過自
   shall in any event not be more than ten years from 12 June           2019年6月12日（即本公司於聯交所上
   2019 (being the date of listing of the Company on the Stock          市的日期）起十年。
   Exchange).

29. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                           29. 資本風險管理

    The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in            本集團管理其資本以確保本集團實體
    the Group will be able to continue as a going concern               能夠持續經營，同時通過優化債務與
    while maximising the return to shareholders through the             股本間的平衡為股東帶來最大回報。
    optimisation of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s            本集團的整體戰略由往年起保持不
    overall strategy remains unchanged from prior year.                 變。


    The capital structure of the Group consists of equity               本集團的資本結構由本集團擁有人應
    attributable to owners of the Group, comprising capital and         佔權益（包括綜合權益變動表所披露
    retained profits as disclosed in the consolidated statement of      的股本及留存利潤）組成。
    changes in equity.


    The directors of the Company review the capital structure           經計及資本成本及與各類資本相關的
    on a semi-annual basis. As part of this review, the directors       風險，本集團董事每半年檢討資本結
    consider the cost of capital and the risks associated with          構。根據本集團董事的推薦意見，本
    each class of capital. Based on recommendations of the              集團將通過派付股息、發行新股及回
    directors, the Group will balance its overall capital structure     購股份以及發行新債或贖回現有債務
    through the payment of dividends, new share issues and              以平衡其整體資本結構。
    share buy-backs as well as the issue of new debt or the
    redemption of existing debt.

      37. RETIREMENT BENEFITS SCHEMES                                       37. 退休福利計劃

           During the year ended 31 December 2019, the employees of           截至2019年12月31日止年度，中國
           the PRC subsidiaries are members of the state-management           附屬公司的僱員為由中國政府運作受
           retirement benefits scheme operated by the PRC                     國家管理的退休福利計劃成員。本集
           government. The Group is required to contribute a specified        團須將薪酬開支按各地方政府機關制
           percentage of payroll costs as determined by respective            定的比例向退休福利計劃作出供款，
           local government authority to the retirement benefits scheme       以為福利提供資金。本集團有關退休
           to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the Group with        福利計劃的唯一責任是根據計劃作出
           respect to the retirement benefits scheme is to make the           規定的供款。
           specified contribution under the scheme.

           The Group also operates a Mandatory Provident Fund                 本集團為所有香港僱員均參與強制性
           Scheme for all employees in Hong Kong. The assets of the           公積金計劃。該計劃的資產與本集團
           scheme are held separately from those of the Group, in funds       的資產分開持有，並由獨立信託人控
           under the control of trustees. The Group contributes a certain     制的基金管理。本集團及僱員在該計
           percentage of the relevant payroll costs to the scheme, which      劃下都須按相關薪金成本若干百分比
           contribution is matched by the employees.                          供款。

           The amounts of contributions made by the Group in respect          本集團於年內就退休福利計劃作出的
           of the retirement benefit scheme during the year are disclosed     供款金額披露於附註11及13。
           in notes 11 and 13.

      38. CONTINGENT LIABILITIES                                            38. 或有負債

           During the year ended 31 December 2019, certain                    截至2019年12月31日止年度，本集
           subsidiaries of the Group failed to make full contributions to     團的若干附屬公司未能根據相關法規
           the housing provident fund for their employees in accordance       及規定為其僱員就住房公積金作出全
           with the relevant regulations and provisions. Based on the         額供款。基於該等集團實體僱員的實
           actual salaries of the employees of these group entities, the      際薪資，未繳足的住房公積金供款約

40. EVENTS AFTER THE REPORTING PERIOD                               40. 結算日後事項

   In early 2020, due to an outbreak of novel coronavirus             於2020年初，由於中國內地爆發新型
   (COVID-19) in mainland China (the “Epidemic”) and the              冠狀病毒(COVID-19)（「疫情」）並於其
   subsequent quarantine measures imposed by the PRC                  後中國政府採取檢疫隔離措施，本集
   government, the physical classes of our schools located            團自2020年2月以來已按照中國政府
   in mainland China had been temporarily suspended since             指示暫緩位於中國內地學校的綫下實
   February 2020 according to the instruction of the PRC              體教學。
   government.


   Up to the date of this report, the physical classes of our         直至本報告日期，我們於中國內地的
   schools in mainland China are yet to be fully resumed in           學校尚未全部正常開展綫下實體教
   a normal way. Following the situation of the Epidemic in           學，但隨著近期中國內地的疫情遂漸
   mainland China gradually improved recently, the Directors          好轉，董事預計本集團的中國內地學
   expected that the physical classes of our schools located          校將遂步恢復綫下實體教學。而本集
   in mainland China would gradually resume. The Group will           團將於取得中國政府有關恢復教育服
   fulfill our contractual obligations with students by launching     務的指示後進行補課，以履行對學生
   make-up classes after receiving the instruction from the           的合約責任。此外，截至目前本集團
   PRC government for resumption of the education services.           尚未收到已報名學生退學及退款申
   Moreover, no request of withdrawal and refund were received        請，但本集團已明確制定各業務分部
   from our students up to now and the Group also has a               項下就學生退學及退款政策。董事認
   clear students’ withdrawal and refund policy in place under        為上述因應疫情所產生的財務影響將
   each business segment. In the opinion of the Directors, the        不屬重大。
   financial impact arising from the above due to the Epidemic
   will not be significant.


   As the situation of the Epidemic remains uncertain as at the       由於疫情於綜合財務報表獲授權刊發
   date of the consolidated financial statements are authorised       日期仍未明朗，董事認為，疫情對招
   for issue, the Directors considered that the impact of             收新生的影響未能合理估算，且可能
   admission for new students due to the Epidemic cannot be           對本集團於2020年上半年及全年的表
   reasonably estimated and it may affect the performance of          現產生影響。
   the Group for the first half and full year of 2020.
